# CLAUDE.md §2 Artifact Pointer Lineage

This file is the relocated, byte-preserving reference body for root `CLAUDE.md` §2.
It is process-substrate, loaded on demand when exact artifact lineage is needed.
Root `CLAUDE.md` keeps the operative pointer index so every session avoids loading this lineage by default.
